EXACT 30 SECOND VIDEO. Seedance 2.5. Vertical 9:16. Audio on. One continuous video.

American man about 40, about 6'5" tall, heavy-set with a big round belly, fair sunburnt skin with freckles, long thick ginger-red beard reaching his chest, short ginger hair, small light-blue eyes, calm deadpan face. Same weathered brown leather cowboy hat, red-and-black buffalo-check flannel shirt with rolled sleeves, light-blue faded jeans, brown belt with a big oval silver buckle, worn brown cowboy boots. Never change his face, beard, hat, outfit, body size or age. Only one Big Earl in the video.

PLACE: a busy roadside BBQ joint outside Austin, Texas, around noon: black smoker trailer puffing smoke, hand-painted sign "SMOKEY JOE'S PIT", long line of locals, red picnic tables, gravel lot with pickup trucks.

CAMERA: Raw real iPhone video filmed by Earl's friend (never seen). It starts inside Earl's old pickup truck parked outside, then follows him in. Handheld, natural shake, real autofocus, natural light, true-to-life colors. Looks like a real video posted on social media, not a movie.

0-3s — HOOK
Inside Earl's old pickup truck parked outside the place, Earl sits behind the wheel and looks over at the phone held by his friend in the passenger seat.
DIALOGUE 0-3s — BIG EARL: "They say this is the best brisket in Texas. We'll see."

3-10s — THE ORDER
They get out and walk in. The phone turns to a tough older man with a grey moustache and black apron, slicing a huge dark-crusted brisket.
DIALOGUE 4-6s — PITMASTER: "What can I get you, big man?"
DIALOGUE 6-8s — BIG EARL: "The whole thing."
DIALOGUE 8-10s — PITMASTER: "The whole brisket?"
Earl slowly tips his hat.

10-22s — THE TASTE
The whole brisket on butcher paper with white bread and pickles; he tears off a thick juicy slice with his hands and takes a big slow bite, juice in his beard. Someone nearby laughs out loud. The friend steps closer and the autofocus hunts for a second.
DIALOGUE 17-19s — FRIEND (off camera, laughing): "Well? Say something!"

22-30s — PUNCHLINE
Earl nods slowly twice and looks into the phone. Completely deadpan.
DIALOGUE 23-26s — BIG EARL: "Texas... you passed."
DIALOGUE 27-29s — BIG EARL: "State one of fifty. One more, please."
The video ends on Earl's calm face.

SOUND: smoker fire crackling, knife on wood, crowd chatter, gravel under boots. Big Earl's voice: deep, slow, calm American male voice with a light Texas drawl. All lines spoken clearly with lip-sync, in this order. Dialogue louder than background sound. No music.
